{"session": "01a0f762-8e99-71e3-b9ab-f624a605db34", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T12:27:22.223Z", "call": {"type": "custom_tool_call", "id": "ctc_0a7f4dda9444844e016abe51a7b0f087d2943d929e3ad6c8e5", "status": "completed", "call_id": "call_pidhT3SblhKMADBzp3HvlxqZ", "name": "exec", "input": "text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:\"cat /private/var/folders/cs/vgp_x_353zd9frkjpysp7zdw0000gn/T/kntnt-native-session-1jvn8o6u/scratch/write-source-check-k_jvhkee/report.md\",max_output_tokens:18000}));\n", "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f762-9069-76e1-a2d8-5de9225b568d", "create_time": 1790857637.122066}}}
{"session": "01a0f762-8e99-71e3-b9ab-f624a605db34", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T12:27:22.314Z", "result": {"type": "custom_tool_call_output", "id": "ctco_01a0f76f-014a-7682-81b8-672919aa504c", "call_id": "call_pidhT3SblhKMADBzp3HvlxqZ", "output": [{"type": "input_text", "text": "Script completed\nWall time 0.1 seconds\nOutput:\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "{\"chunk_id\":\"046aa6\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.00000175,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":8752,\"output\":\"# Source comparison report\\n\\nCompared the complete supplied `source.md` with the complete `draft.md`, including its title, byline, introduction and four subheadings. Source language: Swedish. Draft language: British English. The material expressly identifies the debate as fictional. This report evaluates support and meaning, not general editorial or mechanical quality.\\n\\n## Complete claim accounting\\n\\nEach draft passage is reproduced below. Source passages are quoted in Swedish, with the qualifications governing their claims retained. Where a passage is the commissioned author’s argument or advocacy, that status is distinguished from an independently established fact.\\n\\n### Title, introduction and attribution\\n\\n**1. Draft:** “Lervik should trial telephone and web bookings”\\n\\n**Source:** “Skriv en debattartikel för invånarna i fiktiva Lervik. Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Commissioned advocacy. “Lervik” is a geographical shorthand for the municipality addressed by the argument; the opening and ending identify the executive committee as the decision maker. Telephone and web are the two specified booking routes. The title does not claim a trial has been approved or begun. Supported.\\n\\n**2. Draft:** “Removing telephone booking would settle how Lervik’s association premises can be booked while important questions remain unanswered.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september.” And: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” And: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “Would” keeps removal hypothetical. Removal would decide the availability of a booking route; the description of that as settling how the premises can be booked is the author’s argument about the proposal’s consequence. “Important” is the author’s valuation of the unanswered matters, rather than a measurement or externally attributed evaluation. The missing measurements and the proposed investigation supply its factual basis. No assertion that every possible operational detail would thereby be settled is required by the contextual reading. Supported argument.\\n\\n**3. Draft:** “The pilot’s figures and the gaps in the cost evidence explain why a further trial is needed.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon. Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.” Also: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” And the commissioned thesis quoted in item 1.\\n\\n**Comparison:** The existence of figures and the absence of specified cost evidence are supported. The conclusion that these warrant another trial is the author’s reasoning, consistent with the brief. It is not an attributed empirical finding that a trial is objectively necessary. Supported.\\n\\n**4. Draft:** “By Sanna Ek”\\n\\n**Source:** “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Exact author attribution. The text does not invent a further qualification, affiliation or biography. Supported.\\n\\n**5. Draft:** “As spokesperson for Öppna beslut, I am asking Lervik’s municipal executive committee to postpone a permanent move to online-only booking of association premises.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The author’s role, association name, municipality, decision-making body, permanence of the proposed change and requested postponement are retained. First-person advocacy is authorised by the assigned author and thesis. In this material the digital route is the web route contrasted with telephone booking. “Online-only” denotes that route in context; it does not introduce a claim about a separate technological system. “Association premises” denotes the bookable association-use accommodation, without asserting association ownership. Supported; contextual terminology is addressed further below.\\n\\n**6. Draft:** “We propose six months with both telephone and web bookings at all seven premises.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” With the named routes supplied by: “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “We” voices the association’s proposal through its commissioned spokesperson. Duration, inclusion of all seven locations and preservation of both routes match. It remains a proposal; the sentence does not assert funding, approval or implementation. Supported.\\n\\n**7. Draft:** “The committee should first be able to weigh staff costs against value for users.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Also: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The draft states Sanna Ek’s commissioned position directly rather than reporting it in the third person. “First” retains the required order. The committee is the municipal decision-making body explicitly charged in the source with considering cost and deciding the channel question. “Staff costs” renders the work costs of the administration discussed in this context; it does not claim an amount. “Should” expresses advocacy and does not assert a legal duty. Supported.\\n\\n### First subheading and pilot evidence\\n\\n**8. Draft:** “Telephone bookings do not prove users cannot book online”\\n\\n**Source:** “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras.” Also: “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The subheading preserves the distinction between using telephone booking and being unable to use the web. Its plural form generalises the supplied statement about a booking without adding a population figure. It does not assert that all telephone users can book online. Supported.\\n\\n**9. Draft:** “The municipality’s report, *Bokning av föreningslokaler*, dated 8 April 2026, records 96 web bookings and 24 telephone bookings during an eight-week trial at two premises.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Municipality, report title, report date, trial duration, two-location scope, both booking counts and route attribution match. The figures are explicitly attributed to the report at the point of use. The draft does not turn the report’s publication date into the trial’s starting or ending date. “Records” describes the report’s counts, not a claim about unique individuals. Supported.\\n\\n**10. Draft:** “These are bookings, not individual users.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “These” refers to the immediately preceding 96 and 24. The unit is bookings. “Individual users” distinguishes persons from booking events in this context; it does not say the counts equal distinct people or associations. The source’s counting qualification is retained beside the figures. Supported.\\n\\n**11. Draft:** “The report did not measure age, functional ability or digital familiarity.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The subject remains the report and its measures. All three exclusions remain. Past tense situates this reported pilot rather than changing what was measured. “Functional ability” preserves ability rather than replacing it with diagnosed disability or a claim about disability prevalence. “Digital familiarity” preserves the experience/familiarity dimension of “digital vana”; it does not turn it into inability. Supported.\\n\\n**12. Draft:** “Its telephone figures therefore cannot tell us what proportion of residents cannot book online.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The source’s inferential limit, resident-population scope and inability question remain. “Therefore” rests on the immediately preceding measurement exclusions, as in the Swedish. “Cannot tell us” states the insufficiency of these figures; it does not claim that there are no residents unable to use the web. Supported.\\n\\n**13. Draft:** “A telephone booking gives us a reason to investigate why that route is still used; it does not establish that someone cannot use the web.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** This is the supplied author’s position in paraphrase, with its clauses reordered. The continued use of telephone booking, reason for investigation and rejection of a proof of inability remain. It attributes no motive to users and does not assert that they possess web skills. “Someone” retains the indefinite singular, without gender information. Supported.\\n\\n### Second subheading and administrative objection\\n\\n**14. Draft:** “Duplicate administration deserves a measured answer”\\n\\n**Source:** “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden. Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna. Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” Also: “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “Duplicate administration” names the supplied objection; “deserves a measured answer” is the author’s argument for gathering the missing measurements. It neither establishes a measured saving nor replaces the administration’s stated concern with a motive about telephone users. Supported.\\n\\n**15. Draft:** “The officers’ report for the committee’s 18 June meeting proposes ending telephone bookings at all seven premises from September.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “Officers’ report” identifies the administrative report/recommendation, rather than the pilot report or a decision taken by the committee. Meeting date, proposal status, route, all-seven scope and proposed September commencement match. Neither the source nor draft here adds a year. The sentence does not say the meeting occurred, that the committee voted for removal or that removal happened. Supported.\\n\\n**16. Draft:** “Its stated reason is to spare staff from entering information through two workflows.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden.” In context: “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Attribution remains with the officers’ report. Staff, entry of information and two workflows match. “Stated” does not add an insinuation of concealed motives in its surrounding paragraph: the next sentence explicitly takes that objection seriously, and no alternative motive is asserted. Supported.\\n\\n**17. Draft:** “That objection deserves to be taken seriously.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** This is the author’s evaluative response to the supported administrative objection. It does not claim that the administration has demonstrated a particular burden or saving. The commissioned position allows weighing work costs, so this response is consistent with that standpoint. Supported authorial evaluation.\\n\\n**18. Draft:** “But the documents contain neither measurements of staff time nor calculations of financial savings.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The absence is correctly scoped to the documents. The draft does not extend it to all municipal knowledge, all past research or a claim that savings cannot exist. “Staff time” specifies the time relevant to the stated staff-administration concern; the broader supplied absence of any time measurement includes this absence. Both excluded forms of evidence remain. Supported.\\n\\n**19. Draft:** “The committee needs evidence to assess the workload alongside the value to users.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Also: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” And: “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The author argues for information allowing the supplied cost/value assessment. Workload is the time-related dimension the proposed records would address; no quantified workload or equivalence between time and total cost is asserted. “Needs” expresses the author’s decision-making standard, not a legal requirement. Supported argument.\\n\\n**20. Draft:** “A wish to simplify administration cannot supply that evidence.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden. Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “Wish to simplify administration” summarises the report’s stated purpose of avoiding entry in two workflows. The distinction between a stated purpose and measurements supporting its effects is the author’s reasoning. It introduces no unsupported saving, user characteristic or hidden motive. Supported.\\n\\n### Third subheading and proposed investigation\\n\\n**21. Draft:** “A wider trial would inform the decision”\\n\\n**Source:** “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler.” Also: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar. Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” And: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “Wider” has a supplied comparator: seven locations instead of two, with a longer trial. “Would inform” argues that the specified investigation supplies information relevant to the decision. It does not guarantee a particular result, a saving or a causal improvement in booking behaviour. Supported.\\n\\n**22. Draft:** “During our proposed trial, the administration would record the time spent on each booking route and ask users to volunteer their reasons for choosing telephone or web.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar. Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Attribution stays with Öppna beslut’s proposed trial. “Would” avoids presenting its measures as completed or approved. Administration, time by route, requests to users, voluntariness and the telephone/web choice all remain. “Volunteer their reasons” means provide those reasons voluntarily, not volunteer for unpaid administrative work. No measurement of age, disability, proficiency or unique user population is added. Supported.\\n\\n**23. Draft:** “This would give the committee a basis for considering both sides of the decision.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Also the proposed time records and voluntary reasons quoted in item 22; and: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “This” refers to the proposed information gathering, and “both sides” to the work/cost issue and value for users established by the surrounding text. It is the author’s argument about relevance, not a claim that these measurements establish every cost or all user preferences. “A basis” does not assert that the evidence would be exhaustive or conclusive. Supported.\\n\\n**24. Draft:** “Öppna beslut does not claim to have funded or costed the trial.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The association’s absence of a claim is retained. This does not become an assertion that funding or costing definitely has not occurred anywhere. “Funded” and “costed” refer to financing and estimating the cost of this proposed trial. Subject and retrospective modality match. Supported.\\n\\n**25. Draft:** “The executive committee must consider its cost.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The cost is that of the proposed trial, as established by the preceding sentence. “Must consider” expresses the supplied requirement within this proposed decision process; the passage introduces no legal obligation. The committee’s responsibility is retained without inventing a cost figure or claiming available financing. Supported.\\n\\n**26. Draft:** “I do not oppose digital booking; I want the choice of booking routes to be informed.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.” Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” And: “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** The supplied non-opposition becomes first-person expression by the commissioned author. The desire for an informed route choice is an authorial formulation of the supplied inquiry and weighing of costs against user value. It invents no personal experience, prior action or view attributed to another person. Supported.\\n\\n### Final subheading and requested decisions\\n\\n**27. Draft:** “The committee can remove, change or retain a booking route”\\n\\n**Source:** “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Decision maker, possibility rather than obligation, singular channel and all three options are retained. As an orienting subheading, the wording names the endpoint of the process developed under it; the paragraph explicitly says “Afterwards,” preserving the source’s ordering. It does not claim that any option has already been selected. Supported.\\n\\n**28. Draft:** “The committee should postpone online-only booking and first approve a six-month trial at all seven premises, keeping telephone and web bookings available and addressing the unresolved cost.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” And: “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket. Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** Commissioned advocacy names the requested committee actions, the six-month duration, all-seven scope and preservation of both routes. “Approve” names the committee action needed to authorise the proposed trial; it does not report an approval. “Addressing the unresolved cost” preserves the costing/funding uncertainty and the committee’s responsibility; it does not claim that the trial is free, funded or cost-neutral. The requested decision is supported.\\n\\n**29. Draft:** “Afterwards, it can decide whether either route should be removed, changed or retained.”\\n\\n**Source:** “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” In the proposal’s context: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar. Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”\\n\\n**Comparison:** “Afterwards” retains the decision’s position after the proposed investigation and cost consideration. “It” denotes the committee. “Either route” identifies either one of the two routes retained in the proposed trial; it does not assert that both must be removed or that removal is the expected result. Possibility, subject and all three options remain. Supported.\\n\\n## Pronoun and natural-gender accounting\\n\\nIdentifying a referent is kept separate from the grammatical person, number and natural gender conveyed by a form.\\n\\n| Draft form and location | Person and number conveyed | Natural gender conveyed | Source comparison |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| “I” in “I am asking” | First-person singular | None | The brief assigns authorship to Sanna Ek and the spokesperson role. The source’s later “Hon” conveys female natural gender, but English “I” does not reproduce or contradict that feature. |\\n| “We” in “We propose”; “our” in “our proposed trial” | First-person plural/collective | None | “Öppna beslut föreslår” identifies the association as the proposing party and “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut” establishes representation. The collective voice refers to that association, without claiming a membership count or identifying members’ genders. |\\n| “These” in “These are bookings” | Plural demonstrative | None | Refers to the 96 and 24 counts: “Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon. Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.” The referents are booking counts, not people. |\\n| “Its” in “Its telephone figures” | Third-person singular possessive | None | Refers to the pilot report. The Swedish “Den” in “Den mäter inte …” and noun “rapporten” have grammatical common gender; they do not give the report human natural gender. English “its” likewise adds none. |\\n| “us” in “cannot tell us” and “gives us a reason” | First-person plural | None | The author addresses Lervik’s residents under “Skriv en debattartikel för invånarna i fiktiva Lervik.” Inclusive “us” concerns the conclusion available from the figures and the reason to inquire; it invents no survey, shared experience, unanimous opinion or demographic property of the readers. |\\n| “that route” and “it” in “it does not establish” | Singular demonstrative; third-person singular | None | “That route” refers to telephone booking; “it” refers to a telephone booking. Source: “Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben.” No person or population is assigned a gender. |\\n| “someone” | Indefinite singular | None | Matches “någon”; neither form specifies natural gender, age or identity. A singular indefinite possibility does not establish a count of actual people unable to use the web. |\\n| “Its” in “Its stated reason” | Third-person singular possessive | None | Refers to “Tjänsteutlåtandet”, not the pilot report. The Swedish noun has grammatical neuter gender. Neither the Swedish noun nor English possessive asserts human natural gender. |\\n| “That objection”, “that evidence” | Singular demonstratives | None | Refer to duplicate administration and the evidence needed for workload/user-value assessment respectively. Both are nonhuman abstract referents. |\\n| “their” in “their reasons” | Third-person plural possessive in this sentence | None | Antecedent is plural “users”. Source: “be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” The source’s “de” is third-person plural and specifies no natural genders. |\\n| “This” in “This would give” | Singular demonstrative referring to the preceding proposed process | None | Refers to recording time and asking for voluntary reasons. It does not introduce a person, natural gender or new actor. |\\n| “its” in “consider its cost” | Third-person singular possessive | None | Refers to the proposed trial: “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket. Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.” Swedish “försöket” is grammatically neuter, without natural gender. |\\n| Both “I” forms in “I do not oppose …; I want …” | First-person singular | None | The non-opposition and cost/value/inquiry position are Sanna Ek’s. Source “Hon” specifies third-person singular female reference, while the commissioned first-person conversion removes third-person presentation and leaves natural gender unstated. No incompatible gender is asserted. |\\n| “it” in “Afterwards, it can decide” | Third-person singular | None | Refers to the committee. Source: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra …”. Swedish “den” is singular grammatical common gender for the body, not an assertion of a human female decision maker. |\\n| “either route” | Distributive singular choice from the two routes | None | Matches the unspecified single channel in “om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas”, in the telephone/web context. No natural-gender information is involved. |\\n\\nNo pronoun introduces unsupported natural gender, a population size, a new individual speaker or an unsupported personal experience. The source’s explicit female third-person presentation of Sanna Ek is not contradicted by gender-neutral English first-person expression.\\n\\n## Cross-language support checks and countercase testing\\n\\nNo directly quoted speech appears in the draft. Consequently there are no direct-speech translation findings. The source statement attributed to Sanna Ek is paraphrased as her own argument, and its meaning is accounted for in items 8, 13 and 26.\\n\\nThe following potentially material terminology and scope differences were checked in both directions. They do not establish defects in this draft’s contextual meaning.\\n\\n- **“Föreningslokaler” / “association premises”:** Both identify the accommodation available for association booking in this municipal proposal. A possible difference would be municipally owned rooms used by associations rather than property owned by an association. The Swedish source allows that case, but the English draft also allows it: “association” specifies the use/category of these bookable premises and asserts no owner. Conversely, association-owned accommodation would not be excluded by the Swedish term alone, and the draft adds no such ownership assertion. Multiple separately bookable rooms in a single building are compatible with the source; “premises” does not require separate freestanding buildings or include a stated area of land. The draft does not convert the two or seven bookable locations into a count of buildings, owners or associations. No concrete compatible case establishes a contextual mismatch.\\n- **“Enbart digital bokning” / “online-only booking”:** In abstraction, digital and online can have different technological extensions. A hypothetical offline digital booking method would illustrate that abstract difference. This material, however, identifies the relevant two channels as telephone and web: “först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb” and “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration”. The draft’s phrase identifies the remaining web booking route in that same two-route proposal; it does not separately claim that every digital transaction requires internet connectivity. Adding a separate offline digital route does not make the draft assert a different result about the two routes under discussion. Conversely, online-only booking in this context is digital booking by the web route. No concrete case in which the contextual claims differ was identified.\\n- **“Funktionsförmåga” / “functional ability”:** Both name functional capacity as the unmeasured dimension. A case in which someone has a diagnosed disability but retains capacity to make a booking does not create a difference: neither passage substitutes disability status for ability. Conversely, impaired functional ability without a diagnosis is within both terms. No narrower diagnostic or bodily category is introduced.\\n- **“Digital vana” / “digital familiarity”:** Both concern familiarity/experience with digital use. Being unfamiliar while nevertheless capable of completing a booking, or experienced but unable to complete a particular booking, remains compatible with both passages. Neither passage treats this measure as the same as capacity, diagnosis or age. No contrary contextual case was identified.\\n- **“Unika personer” / “individual users”:** A repeat user making many bookings illustrates the central distinction in both passages: the figures count booking events, rather than distinct individuals. A report might additionally contain an unrelated count of organisations without changing what the 96 and 24 mean; the draft’s “These” is limited to those figures. It does not assert that no other information appears anywhere in the report. No contrary case is created by that possibility.\\n- **“Någon tidsmätning … finns inte i handlingarna” / no “measurements of staff time” in the documents:** The English statement is limited to the staff time relevant to the objection. A possible English-true/Swedish-false case would be documents containing only measurements of users’ waiting time. It is excluded by the supplied “Någon tidsmätning … finns inte i handlingarna.” The reverse case—no time measurement of any kind but a staff-time measurement—cannot hold because staff time is time. Thus the draft’s supported narrower claim is not a defect.\\n- **“Gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat” / “does not claim to have funded or costed”:** Financing by the association and its estimating the trial’s cost are the corresponding actions. If someone else had funded or costed the trial, both passages could still be true. If the association had done either action without claiming it, both passages could likewise still be true. Neither supplies the stronger assertion that financing or costing never occurred. No epistemic change was found.\\n- **“Därefter … en kanal” / “Afterwards … either route”:** Both make a later committee decision possible about one of the relevant routes. The draft neither commits the committee to removal nor claims that a particular route must be selected. A decision to retain telephone or alter web booking fits both, as does a decision concerning the other route. No changed option or chronology was found.\\n\\n## Findings\\n\\n**Source-support findings:** None. Every factual or attributed claim has support identified above, and the remaining evaluative and prospective claims are supported as the commissioned author’s argument or advocacy. Figures retain report attribution and counting exclusions. The proposed removal remains a proposal. The administration’s objection is preserved. No saving, population inference, legal duty, completed trial, financing certainty, personal experience or alternative motive is invented.\\n\\n**Translation findings:** None. There is no translated direct quotation, and no unsupported change of meaning was identified in the paraphrase or other cross-language factual wording.\\n\\n**Smallest supported repairs:** No repairs are required on source-support grounds. This conclusion makes no general editorial or mechanical assessment.\\n\\n**Completion status:** Complete; the whole source and whole draft, including every heading, have been compared. **Unresolved findings:** None.\\n\"}"}], "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f762-9069-76e1-a2d8-5de9225b568d", "create_time": 1790857642.314779}}}

{"session": "01a0f767-17fe-7320-95f9-fd16f2fb882e", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T12:18:52.690Z", "result": {"type": "custom_tool_call_output", "id": "ctco_01a0f767-3a92-72f3-a9b3-37a7e25af835", "call_id": "call_ZYGM4GAzb1iXBmb4QZCTFyqK", "output": [{"type": "input_text", "text": "Script completed\nWall time 0.1 seconds\nOutput:\n"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "{\"file\":0,\"result\":{\"status\":\"fulfilled\",\"value\":{\"chunk_id\":\"3c98c0\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000001292,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":673,\"output\":\"# Opinion brief and source\\n\\nSYNTHETIC EVALUATION MATERIAL. Fictional policy debate, not legal advice or a real municipal process. Source language: Swedish.\\n\\n## Brief\\n\\nSkriv en debattartikel för invånarna i fiktiva Lervik. Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb. En tydlig, sakligt skarp text på ungefär 400 ord. Markdown med orienterande rubriker. Ingen kommersiell CTA.\\n\\n## Underlag\\n\\nKommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon. Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.\\n\\nTjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september. Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden. Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna. Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.\\n\\nÖppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar. Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb. Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket. Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.\\n\\nSanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras. Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna. Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.\\n\\n## Boundaries / judging\\n\\nInga lagkrav, protester, diskrimineringsfall, partipolitiska motiv eller besparingar är belagda. Siffror ska tillskrivas rapporten där de bär argumentet. Författaren ska kunna kritisera beslutsunderlaget skarpt utan falska motiv eller tandlös neutralisering. Slutet ska namnge kommunstyrelsens möjliga beslut, med kostnadsosäkerheten bevarad.\\n\"}}}"}, {"type": "input_text", "text": "{\"file\":1,\"result\":{\"status\":\"fulfilled\",\"value\":{\"chunk_id\":\"70c81a\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000002125,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":654,\"output\":\"# Lervik should trial telephone and web bookings\\n\\nRemoving telephone booking would settle how Lervik’s association premises can be booked while important questions remain unanswered. The pilot’s figures and the gaps in the cost evidence explain why a further trial is needed.\\n\\nBy Sanna Ek\\n\\nAs spokesperson for Öppna beslut, I am asking Lervik’s municipal executive committee to postpone a permanent move to online-only booking of association premises. We propose six months with both telephone and web bookings at all seven premises. The committee should first be able to weigh staff costs against value for users.\\n\\n## Telephone bookings do not prove users cannot book online\\n\\nThe municipality’s report, *Bokning av föreningslokaler*, dated 8 April 2026, records 96 web bookings and 24 telephone bookings during an eight-week trial at two premises. These are bookings, not individual users.\\n\\nThe report did not measure age, functional ability or digital familiarity. Its telephone figures therefore cannot tell us what proportion of residents cannot book online. A telephone booking gives us a reason to investigate why that route is still used; it does not establish that someone cannot use the web.\\n\\n## Duplicate administration deserves a measured answer\\n\\nThe officers’ report for the committee’s 18 June meeting proposes ending telephone bookings at all seven premises from September. Its stated reason is to spare staff from entering information through two workflows.\\n\\nThat objection deserves to be taken seriously. But the documents contain neither measurements of staff time nor calculations of financial savings. The committee needs evidence to assess the workload alongside the value to users. A wish to simplify administration cannot supply that evidence.\\n\\n## A wider trial would inform the decision\\n\\nDuring our proposed trial, the administration would record the time spent on each booking route and ask users to volunteer their reasons for choosing telephone or web. This would give the committee a basis for considering both sides of the decision.\\n\\nÖppna beslut does not claim to have funded or costed the trial. The executive committee must consider its cost. I do not oppose digital booking; I want the choice of booking routes to be informed.\\n\\n## The committee can remove, change or retain a booking route\\n\\nThe committee should postpone online-only booking and first approve a six-month trial at all seven premises, keeping telephone and web bookings available and addressing the unresolved cost. Afterwards, it can decide whether either route should be removed, changed or retained.\\n\"}}}"}], "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f767-182d-7fb3-ac15-f4b818360c85", "create_time": 1790857132.690786}}}

{"session": "01a0f767-17fe-7320-95f9-fd16f2fb882e", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T12:26:03.444Z", "call": {"type": "custom_tool_call", "id": "ctc_0ced5cb3341fc0af016abe5036d4e487d28264bb0ea28b7cc9", "status": "completed", "call_id": "call_Pc0pj0lwYf6V3SRo3iIzKoV1", "name": "exec", "input": "text(await tools.apply_patch(\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Add File: /private/var/folders/cs/vgp_x_353zd9frkjpysp7zdw0000gn/T/kntnt-native-session-1jvn8o6u/scratch/write-source-check-k_jvhkee/report.md\\n+# Source comparison report\\n+\\n+Compared the complete supplied `source.md` with the complete `draft.md`, including its title, byline, introduction and four subheadings. Source language: Swedish. Draft language: British English. The material expressly identifies the debate as fictional. This report evaluates support and meaning, not general editorial or mechanical quality.\\n+\\n+## Complete claim accounting\\n+\\n+Each draft passage is reproduced below. Source passages are quoted in Swedish, with the qualifications governing their claims retained. Where a passage is the commissioned author’s argument or advocacy, that status is distinguished from an independently established fact.\\n+\\n+### Title, introduction and attribution\\n+\\n+**1. Draft:** “Lervik should trial telephone and web bookings”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Skriv en debattartikel för invånarna i fiktiva Lervik. Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** Commissioned advocacy. “Lervik” is a geographical shorthand for the municipality addressed by the argument; the opening and ending identify the executive committee as the decision maker. Telephone and web are the two specified booking routes. The title does not claim a trial has been approved or begun. Supported.\\n+\\n+**2. Draft:** “Removing telephone booking would settle how Lervik’s association premises can be booked while important questions remain unanswered.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september.” And: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” And: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** “Would” keeps removal hypothetical. Removal would decide the availability of a booking route; the description of that as settling how the premises can be booked is the author’s argument about the proposal’s consequence. “Important” is the author’s valuation of the unanswered matters, rather than a measurement or externally attributed evaluation. The missing measurements and the proposed investigation supply its factual basis. No assertion that every possible operational detail would thereby be settled is required by the contextual reading. Supported argument.\\n+\\n+**3. Draft:** “The pilot’s figures and the gaps in the cost evidence explain why a further trial is needed.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon. Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.” Also: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” And the commissioned thesis quoted in item 1.\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The existence of figures and the absence of specified cost evidence are supported. The conclusion that these warrant another trial is the author’s reasoning, consistent with the brief. It is not an attributed empirical finding that a trial is objectively necessary. Supported.\\n+\\n+**4. Draft:** “By Sanna Ek”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** Exact author attribution. The text does not invent a further qualification, affiliation or biography. Supported.\\n+\\n+**5. Draft:** “As spokesperson for Öppna beslut, I am asking Lervik’s municipal executive committee to postpone a permanent move to online-only booking of association premises.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The author’s role, association name, municipality, decision-making body, permanence of the proposed change and requested postponement are retained. First-person advocacy is authorised by the assigned author and thesis. In this material the digital route is the web route contrasted with telephone booking. “Online-only” denotes that route in context; it does not introduce a claim about a separate technological system. “Association premises” denotes the bookable association-use accommodation, without asserting association ownership. Supported; contextual terminology is addressed further below.\\n+\\n+**6. Draft:** “We propose six months with both telephone and web bookings at all seven premises.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” With the named routes supplied by: “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** “We” voices the association’s proposal through its commissioned spokesperson. Duration, inclusion of all seven locations and preservation of both routes match. It remains a proposal; the sentence does not assert funding, approval or implementation. Supported.\\n+\\n+**7. Draft:** “The committee should first be able to weigh staff costs against value for users.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Also: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The draft states Sanna Ek’s commissioned position directly rather than reporting it in the third person. “First” retains the required order. The committee is the municipal decision-making body explicitly charged in the source with considering cost and deciding the channel question. “Staff costs” renders the work costs of the administration discussed in this context; it does not claim an amount. “Should” expresses advocacy and does not assert a legal duty. Supported.\\n+\\n+### First subheading and pilot evidence\\n+\\n+**8. Draft:** “Telephone bookings do not prove users cannot book online”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras.” Also: “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The subheading preserves the distinction between using telephone booking and being unable to use the web. Its plural form generalises the supplied statement about a booking without adding a population figure. It does not assert that all telephone users can book online. Supported.\\n+\\n+**9. Draft:** “The municipality’s report, *Bokning av föreningslokaler*, dated 8 April 2026, records 96 web bookings and 24 telephone bookings during an eight-week trial at two premises.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** Municipality, report title, report date, trial duration, two-location scope, both booking counts and route attribution match. The figures are explicitly attributed to the report at the point of use. The draft does not turn the report’s publication date into the trial’s starting or ending date. “Records” describes the report’s counts, not a claim about unique individuals. Supported.\\n+\\n+**10. Draft:** “These are bookings, not individual users.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** “These” refers to the immediately preceding 96 and 24. The unit is bookings. “Individual users” distinguishes persons from booking events in this context; it does not say the counts equal distinct people or associations. The source’s counting qualification is retained beside the figures. Supported.\\n+\\n+**11. Draft:** “The report did not measure age, functional ability or digital familiarity.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The subject remains the report and its measures. All three exclusions remain. Past tense situates this reported pilot rather than changing what was measured. “Functional ability” preserves ability rather than replacing it with diagnosed disability or a claim about disability prevalence. “Digital familiarity” preserves the experience/familiarity dimension of “digital vana”; it does not turn it into inability. Supported.\\n+\\n+**12. Draft:** “Its telephone figures therefore cannot tell us what proportion of residents cannot book online.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The source’s inferential limit, resident-population scope and inability question remain. “Therefore” rests on the immediately preceding measurement exclusions, as in the Swedish. “Cannot tell us” states the insufficiency of these figures; it does not claim that there are no residents unable to use the web. Supported.\\n+\\n+**13. Draft:** “A telephone booking gives us a reason to investigate why that route is still used; it does not establish that someone cannot use the web.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** This is the supplied author’s position in paraphrase, with its clauses reordered. The continued use of telephone booking, reason for investigation and rejection of a proof of inability remain. It attributes no motive to users and does not assert that they possess web skills. “Someone” retains the indefinite singular, without gender information. Supported.\\n+\\n+### Second subheading and administrative objection\\n+\\n+**14. Draft:** “Duplicate administration deserves a measured answer”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden. Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna. Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” Also: “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** “Duplicate administration” names the supplied objection; “deserves a measured answer” is the author’s argument for gathering the missing measurements. It neither establishes a measured saving nor replaces the administration’s stated concern with a motive about telephone users. Supported.\\n+\\n+**15. Draft:** “The officers’ report for the committee’s 18 June meeting proposes ending telephone bookings at all seven premises from September.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** “Officers’ report” identifies the administrative report/recommendation, rather than the pilot report or a decision taken by the committee. Meeting date, proposal status, route, all-seven scope and proposed September commencement match. Neither the source nor draft here adds a year. The sentence does not say the meeting occurred, that the committee voted for removal or that removal happened. Supported.\\n+\\n+**16. Draft:** “Its stated reason is to spare staff from entering information through two workflows.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden.” In context: “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** Attribution remains with the officers’ report. Staff, entry of information and two workflows match. “Stated” does not add an insinuation of concealed motives in its surrounding paragraph: the next sentence explicitly takes that objection seriously, and no alternative motive is asserted. Supported.\\n+\\n+**17. Draft:** “That objection deserves to be taken seriously.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** This is the author’s evaluative response to the supported administrative objection. It does not claim that the administration has demonstrated a particular burden or saving. The commissioned position allows weighing work costs, so this response is consistent with that standpoint. Supported authorial evaluation.\\n+\\n+**18. Draft:** “But the documents contain neither measurements of staff time nor calculations of financial savings.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The absence is correctly scoped to the documents. The draft does not extend it to all municipal knowledge, all past research or a claim that savings cannot exist. “Staff time” specifies the time relevant to the stated staff-administration concern; the broader supplied absence of any time measurement includes this absence. Both excluded forms of evidence remain. Supported.\\n+\\n+**19. Draft:** “The committee needs evidence to assess the workload alongside the value to users.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Also: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” And: “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The author argues for information allowing the supplied cost/value assessment. Workload is the time-related dimension the proposed records would address; no quantified workload or equivalence between time and total cost is asserted. “Needs” expresses the author’s decision-making standard, not a legal requirement. Supported argument.\\n+\\n+**20. Draft:** “A wish to simplify administration cannot supply that evidence.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden. Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** “Wish to simplify administration” summarises the report’s stated purpose of avoiding entry in two workflows. The distinction between a stated purpose and measurements supporting its effects is the author’s reasoning. It introduces no unsupported saving, user characteristic or hidden motive. Supported.\\n+\\n+### Third subheading and proposed investigation\\n+\\n+**21. Draft:** “A wider trial would inform the decision”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler.” Also: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar. Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” And: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** “Wider” has a supplied comparator: seven locations instead of two, with a longer trial. “Would inform” argues that the specified investigation supplies information relevant to the decision. It does not guarantee a particular result, a saving or a causal improvement in booking behaviour. Supported.\\n+\\n+**22. Draft:** “During our proposed trial, the administration would record the time spent on each booking route and ask users to volunteer their reasons for choosing telephone or web.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar. Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** Attribution stays with Öppna beslut’s proposed trial. “Would” avoids presenting its measures as completed or approved. Administration, time by route, requests to users, voluntariness and the telephone/web choice all remain. “Volunteer their reasons” means provide those reasons voluntarily, not volunteer for unpaid administrative work. No measurement of age, disability, proficiency or unique user population is added. Supported.\\n+\\n+**23. Draft:** “This would give the committee a basis for considering both sides of the decision.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Also the proposed time records and voluntary reasons quoted in item 22; and: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** “This” refers to the proposed information gathering, and “both sides” to the work/cost issue and value for users established by the surrounding text. It is the author’s argument about relevance, not a claim that these measurements establish every cost or all user preferences. “A basis” does not assert that the evidence would be exhaustive or conclusive. Supported.\\n+\\n+**24. Draft:** “Öppna beslut does not claim to have funded or costed the trial.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The association’s absence of a claim is retained. This does not become an assertion that funding or costing definitely has not occurred anywhere. “Funded” and “costed” refer to financing and estimating the cost of this proposed trial. Subject and retrospective modality match. Supported.\\n+\\n+**25. Draft:** “The executive committee must consider its cost.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The cost is that of the proposed trial, as established by the preceding sentence. “Must consider” expresses the supplied requirement within this proposed decision process; the passage introduces no legal obligation. The committee’s responsibility is retained without inventing a cost figure or claiming available financing. Supported.\\n+\\n+**26. Draft:** “I do not oppose digital booking; I want the choice of booking routes to be informed.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.” Also: “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” And: “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** The supplied non-opposition becomes first-person expression by the commissioned author. The desire for an informed route choice is an authorial formulation of the supplied inquiry and weighing of costs against user value. It invents no personal experience, prior action or view attributed to another person. Supported.\\n+\\n+### Final subheading and requested decisions\\n+\\n+**27. Draft:** “The committee can remove, change or retain a booking route”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** Decision maker, possibility rather than obligation, singular channel and all three options are retained. As an orienting subheading, the wording names the endpoint of the process developed under it; the paragraph explicitly says “Afterwards,” preserving the source’s ordering. It does not claim that any option has already been selected. Supported.\\n+\\n+**28. Draft:** “The committee should postpone online-only booking and first approve a six-month trial at all seven premises, keeping telephone and web bookings available and addressing the unresolved cost.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” And: “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket. Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** Commissioned advocacy names the requested committee actions, the six-month duration, all-seven scope and preservation of both routes. “Approve” names the committee action needed to authorise the proposed trial; it does not report an approval. “Addressing the unresolved cost” preserves the costing/funding uncertainty and the committee’s responsibility; it does not claim that the trial is free, funded or cost-neutral. The requested decision is supported.\\n+\\n+**29. Draft:** “Afterwards, it can decide whether either route should be removed, changed or retained.”\\n+\\n+**Source:** “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” In the proposal’s context: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar. Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.”\\n+\\n+**Comparison:** “Afterwards” retains the decision’s position after the proposed investigation and cost consideration. “It” denotes the committee. “Either route” identifies either one of the two routes retained in the proposed trial; it does not assert that both must be removed or that removal is the expected result. Possibility, subject and all three options remain. Supported.\\n+\\n+## Pronoun and natural-gender accounting\\n+\\n+Identifying a referent is kept separate from the grammatical person, number and natural gender conveyed by a form.\\n+\\n+| Draft form and location | Person and number conveyed | Natural gender conveyed | Source comparison |\\n+| --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n+| “I” in “I am asking” | First-person singular | None | The brief assigns authorship to Sanna Ek and the spokesperson role. It authorises this first-person position. The source’s later “Hon” conveys female natural gender, but English “I” does not reproduce or contradict that feature. |\\n+| “We” in “We propose”; “our” in “our proposed trial” | First-person plural/collective | None | “Öppna beslut föreslår” identifies the association as the proposing party and “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut” establishes representation. The collective voice refers to that association, without claiming a membership count or identifying members’ genders. |\\n+| “These” in “These are bookings” | Plural demonstrative | None | Refers to the 96 and 24 counts: “Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon. Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.” The referents are booking counts, not people. |\\n+| “Its” in “Its telephone figures” | Third-person singular possessive | None | Refers to the pilot report. The Swedish “Den” in “Den mäter inte …” and noun “rapporten” have grammatical common gender; they do not give the report human natural gender. English “its” likewise adds none. |\\n+| “us” in “cannot tell us” and “gives us a reason” | First-person plural | None | The author addresses Lervik’s residents under “Skriv en debattartikel för invånarna i fiktiva Lervik.” Inclusive “us” concerns the conclusion available from the figures and the reason to inquire; it invents no survey, shared experience, unanimous opinion or demographic property of the readers. |\\n+| “that route” and “it” in “it does not establish” | Singular demonstrative; third-person singular | None | “That route” refers to telephone booking; “it” refers to a telephone booking. Source: “Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben.” No person or population is assigned a gender. |\\n+| “someone” | Indefinite singular | None | Matches “någon”; neither form specifies natural gender, age or identity. A singular indefinite possibility does not establish a count of actual people unable to use the web. |\\n+| “Its” in “Its stated reason” | Third-person singular possessive | None | Refers to “Tjänsteutlåtandet”, not the pilot report. The Swedish noun has grammatical neuter gender. Neither the Swedish noun nor English possessive asserts human natural gender. |\\n+| “That objection”, “that evidence” | Singular demonstratives | None | Refer to duplicate administration and the evidence needed for workload/user-value assessment respectively. Both are nonhuman abstract referents. |\\n+| “their” in “their reasons” | Third-person plural possessive in this sentence | None | Antecedent is plural “users”. Source: “be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” The source’s “de” is third-person plural and specifies no natural genders. |\\n+| “This” in “This would give” | Singular demonstrative referring to the preceding proposed process | None | Refers to recording time and asking for voluntary reasons. It does not introduce a person, natural gender or new actor. |\\n+| “its” in “consider its cost” | Third-person singular possessive | None | Refers to the proposed trial: “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket. Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.” Swedish “försöket” is grammatically neuter, without natural gender. |\\n+| Both “I” forms in “I do not oppose …; I want …” | First-person singular | None | The non-opposition and cost/value/inquiry position are Sanna Ek’s. Source “Hon” specifies third-person singular female reference, while the commissioned first-person conversion removes third-person presentation and leaves natural gender unstated. No incompatible gender is asserted. |\\n+| “it” in “Afterwards, it can decide” | Third-person singular | None | Refers to the committee. Source: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra …”. Swedish “den” is singular grammatical common gender for the body, not an assertion of a human female decision maker. |\\n+| “either route” | Distributive singular choice from the two routes | None | Matches the unspecified single channel in “om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas”, in the telephone/web context. No natural-gender information is involved. |\\n+\\n+No pronoun introduces unsupported natural gender, a population size, a new individual speaker or an unsupported personal experience. The source’s explicit female third-person presentation of Sanna Ek is not contradicted by gender-neutral English first-person expression.\\n+\\n+## Cross-language support checks and countercase testing\\n+\\n+No directly quoted speech appears in the draft. Consequently there are no direct-speech translation findings. The source statement attributed to Sanna Ek is paraphrased as her own argument, and its meaning is accounted for in items 8, 13 and 26.\\n+\\n+The following potentially material terminology and scope differences were checked in both directions. They do not establish defects in this draft’s contextual meaning.\\n+\\n+- **“Föreningslokaler” / “association premises”:** Both identify the accommodation available for association booking in this municipal proposal. A possible difference would be municipally owned rooms used by associations rather than property owned by an association. The Swedish source allows that case, but the English draft also allows it: “association” specifies the use/category of these bookable premises and asserts no owner. Conversely, association-owned accommodation would not be excluded by the Swedish term alone, and the draft adds no such ownership assertion. Multiple separately bookable rooms in a single building are compatible with the source; “premises” does not require separate freestanding buildings or include a stated area of land. The draft does not convert the two or seven bookable locations into a count of buildings, owners or associations. No concrete compatible case establishes a contextual mismatch.\\n+- **“Enbart digital bokning” / “online-only booking”:** In abstraction, digital and online can have different technological extensions. A hypothetical offline digital booking method would illustrate that abstract difference. This material, however, identifies the relevant two channels as telephone and web: “först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb” and “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration”. The draft’s phrase identifies the remaining web booking route in that same two-route proposal; it does not separately claim that every digital transaction requires internet connectivity. Adding a separate offline digital route does not make the draft assert a different result about the two routes under discussion. Conversely, online-only booking in this context is digital booking by the web route. No concrete case in which the contextual claims differ was identified.\\n+- **“Funktionsförmåga” / “functional ability”:** Both name functional capacity as the unmeasured dimension. A case in which someone has a diagnosed disability but retains capacity to make a booking does not create a difference: neither passage substitutes disability status for ability. Conversely, impaired functional ability without a diagnosis is within both terms. No narrower diagnostic or bodily category is introduced.\\n+- **“Digital vana” / “digital familiarity”:** Both concern familiarity/experience with digital use. Being unfamiliar while nevertheless capable of completing a booking, or experienced but unable to complete a particular booking, remains compatible with both passages. Neither passage treats this measure as the same as capacity, diagnosis or age. No contrary contextual case was identified.\\n+- **“Unika personer” / “individual users”:** A repeat user making many bookings illustrates the central distinction in both passages: the figures count booking events, rather than distinct individuals. A report might additionally contain an unrelated count of organisations without changing what the 96 and 24 mean; the draft’s “These” is limited to those figures. It does not assert that no other information appears anywhere in the report. No contrary case is created by that possibility.\\n+- **“Någon tidsmätning … finns inte i handlingarna” / no “measurements of staff time” in the documents:** The English statement is limited to the staff time relevant to the objection. A possible English-true/Swedish-false case would be documents containing only measurements of users’ waiting time. It is excluded by the supplied “Någon tidsmätning … finns inte i handlingarna.” The reverse case—no time measurement of any kind but a staff-time measurement—cannot hold because staff time is time. Thus the draft’s supported narrower claim is not a defect.\\n+- **“Gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat” / “does not claim to have funded or costed”:** Financing by the association and its estimating the trial’s cost are the corresponding actions. If someone else had funded or costed the trial, both passages could still be true. If the association had done either action without claiming it, both passages could likewise still be true. Neither supplies the stronger assertion that financing or costing never occurred. No epistemic change was found.\\n+- **“Därefter … en kanal” / “Afterwards … either route”:** Both make a later committee decision possible about one of the relevant routes. The draft neither commits the committee to removal nor claims that a particular route must be selected. A decision to retain telephone or alter web booking fits both, as does a decision concerning the other route. No changed option or chronology was found.\\n+\\n+## Findings\\n+\\n+**Source-support findings:** None. Every factual or attributed claim has support identified above, and the remaining evaluative and prospective claims are supported as the commissioned author’s argument or advocacy. Figures retain report attribution and counting exclusions. The proposed removal remains a proposal. The administration’s objection is preserved. No saving, population inference, legal duty, completed trial, financing certainty, personal experience or alternative motive is invented.\\n+\\n+**Translation findings:** None. There is no translated direct quotation, and no unsupported change of meaning was identified in the paraphrase or other cross-language factual wording.\\n+\\n+**Smallest supported repairs:** No repairs are required on source-support grounds. This conclusion makes no general editorial or mechanical assessment.\\n+\\n+**Completion status:** Complete; the whole source and whole draft, including every heading, have been compared. **Unresolved findings:** None.\\n*** End Patch\"));\n", "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f767-182d-7fb3-ac15-f4b818360c85", "create_time": 1790857244.43262}}}
